Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any fossils module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 138. The projective resolution has length at most 886 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the fossils space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 138 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 886. QED.
